### M2.2d1 caller implementation and corrected overlay assumption

- Source investigation corrects earlier suspected scripted-save claim: address9600 is reused by combatOverlay, combatResults, battleMagic, merchants, inventory and save overlays. byte267d is map/type state (combat temporarily255; live Town2 fixtures type5); pre-keyboard command_loop call9600 is NOT proof of a save call. Do not skip it as autosave. Actual save resource comes from NA/SRTN.INVENTORY offset0xd218 (check_save_controller.c), qualified by its existing fingerprint. Native oracle documents an automatic-entry ABI, but actual additional CE runtime caller remains an audit task, not a confirmed defect. Historical source-address suspicion above is superseded by this finding.
- Actual CE callers: DefaultEventManager::pollEvent invokes Engine::handleAutoSave; Engine::saveAutosaveIfEnabled returns immediately for slot<0; base Engine::loadGameState calls autosave first, but Nox overrides full loadGameState. Shared quit/return dialogs do not invoke a separate save, while event polling still runs the timer. No generic quicksave route found in common/backend keymaps; engine-local quicksave-style callers must obey Nox's manual API policy.
